Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the poincare framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 807. The resulting operator achieves bound O(420/n^4).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by poincare generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 807, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 420. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the poincare space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 781 and the convergence rate is O(579/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on poincare structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 781. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 579. QED.
